DESIGN.md

show
## Concept
The player navigates a lone dot through a dense field of moving obstacles by tapping to generate a temporal bubble that briefly freezes everything within it, letting the dot slip through tight gaps. The tension comes from timing the bubble’s short freeze and managing its cooldown.

## Player
The avatar is a small glowing dot that the player steers by swiping or using directional input. Verbs: move (directional navigation), tap (create temporal bubble). No other abilities.

## Core loop
Every few seconds the player scans the approaching cluster of obstacles, decides when to place a bubble to pause them, and guides the dot through the newly opened path. The loop repeats: observe movement, time a bubble, navigate the freed corridor, then wait for the next obstacle wave.

## Hazards / Obstacles
- **Sweeping bars**: long rectangular obstacles that slide horizontally or vertically, constantly moving along a fixed path and reappearing after exiting the screen. They can trap the dot if not frozen in time.
- **Rotating gears**: circular clusters that spin around a pivot, extending spiked arms that sweep the area. They briefly pause when frozen, but resume rotation once the bubble expires.
- **Pulsing mines**: stationary or slowly drifting mines that emit a short-range pulse when the dot gets too close, causing instant failure unless the pulse is halted by a bubble.
- **Spawned blockers**: occasional bursts of small, fast-moving dots that bounce off walls; they cannot be frozen individually but are stopped while inside the bubble’s radius.

## Score
- **Successful passage**: each time the dot exits a cluster of obstacles after a bubble, the player earns a point.
- **Bubble efficiency**: using a bubble that frees multiple obstacles at once grants a bonus.
- **Combo survival**: navigating three consecutive clusters without a missed bubble adds a streak multiplier.
- **Collectable orbs** (optional): glowing orbs that appear inside frozen zones give extra points when collected.

## Win and lose
The player loses when the dot contacts any active obstacle outside a bubble or runs out of lives. The screen flashes red, and a “Game Over” overlay appears with the final score. The player wins by surviving a predefined number of obstacle waves (e.g., the final wave) or reaching a target score, at which point a celebratory burst of frozen bubbles expands outward, revealing the final score and a “You Win” banner.

## Feel
The game feels frantic yet rhythmic, with a pulse‑driven visual style of neon outlines against a dark void. Each bubble produces a subtle time‑warp distortion and a soft chime, while obstacles emit whooshing tones that halt on freeze, creating a satisfying contrast between motion and stillness.

## Why it's fun
The moment you tap and watch a chaotic swarm freeze in place, carving a perfect path for the dot, delivers an instant “aha!” that makes you want to do it again and again.

TASKS.md

show
1. HTML scaffold and meta.json — generate `index.html` with required header, canvas, restart button, empty script block, and create `meta.json` matching game title, description, controls, tags.
2. Game state objects — declare plain object literals for player, hazards list, score, and phase at top of script with initial values.
3. Main loop implementation — add `startLoop()` with cancelAnimationFrame handling, requestAnimationFrame loop, try/catch, and clear canvas each frame.
4. Input handling — create a `Set` of pressed keys, add `keydown`/`keyup` listeners, and bind R‑key and restart button to a common `reset()` function.
5. Player movement — implement dt‑based physics: velocity updates from arrow keys, caps at MAX_SPEED, friction, and clamp position to canvas bounds.
6. Hazard system — design data structure, spawn rules that respect `SAFE_RADIUS` at start, and dt‑scaled motion for each hazard.
7. Collision detection — check player against hazards each frame; on hit transition `phase` to "gameover" and stop active gameplay.
8. Scoring — increase score on discrete events (e.g., hazard passes), and draw the current score on the canvas top‑left.
9. Rendering — each frame draw background, hazards, player (high‑contrast), and HUD after clearing the canvas.
10. Game‑over overlay — dim the scene, display "Game Over", final score, and restart instructions on the canvas.
11. Observable contract — populate `window.__game` each frame with `state`, `score`, `player` (x, y, visible), and an `objective` sentence.
12. Audio integration — lazily create an `AudioContext` in a `beep()` helper, wrap in try/catch, and trigger sounds on hit, score, and death events.

    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
const restartBtn = document.getElementById('restart');
    function startLoop() {
      if (loopId !== null) cancelAnimationFrame(loopId);
      function tick(now) {
        try {
          const dt = Math.min((now - lastTime) / 1000, 0.1);
          lastTime = now;
          // Clear canvas each frame
          ctx.fillStyle = '#05050c';
          ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);

          console.error('loop error', err);
        }
        loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
      }
      loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
    }
    // Start loop immediately
    startLoop();
  </script>

`DESIGN.md` MUST contain these sections (use these exact headers; the validator checks for them):

## Concept
One or two sentences in your own words. What does the player do, and why is it satisfying?

## Player
Describe what the player controls — the avatar, ship, cursor, paddle, whatever it is. Visually, what is it (one sentence)? List its verbs (move, jump, shoot, place, type). Keep it abstract — no coordinates, no speeds.

## Core loop
What happens every 1–3 seconds of normal play. Be specific about the moment-to-moment experience: what's the player reading, what are they choosing between, what makes them lean forward.

## Hazards / Obstacles
What stands in the player's way. Describe each kind by behavior and threat — "fast horizontal sweepers that telegraph their path", "stationary blocks that explode when shot". Concepts and behaviors, not numbers.

## Score
What discrete events increase the score. Examples: "survived a wave", "collected a pickup", "killed an enemy at long range". Avoid score = elapsed time as the only mechanic.

## Win and lose
When does the player win? When do they lose? What happens on screen the moment it ends.

## Feel
The vibe. Pace (frantic / steady / meditative). Aesthetic direction (neon / pastel / monochrome / retro CRT). Sound character (chiptune / arcade beeps / silent). One short paragraph.

## Why it's fun
One sentence. The single moment that would make a player smile, curse, or shout. What is this game's hook?

Keep DESIGN.md focused on player experience. The next phase (tasks) will translate this into an implementation plan with concrete numbers.

- Replace line 62-63 (start loop immediate) with a block that defines:

```
// === RESET =============================================================
function reset() {
  player.x = W/2;
  player.y = H/2;
  player.vx = 0;
  player.vy = 0;
  player.alive = true;
  hazards = [];
  score = 0;
  phase = "playing";
  keys.clear();
  bubbleActive = false;
  bubbleTimer = 0;
  hazardSpawnTimer = 1.5;
  lastTime = performance.now();
  startLoop();
}

// === INPUT =============================================================
document.addEventListener('keydown', (ev) => {
  if (ev.key === 'r' || ev.key === 'R') { reset(); return; }
  keys.add(ev.key);
  if (ev.key === ' ') ev.preventDefault();
});
document.addEventListener('keyup', (ev) => { keys.delete(ev.key); });
canvas.addEventListener('click', (ev) => {
  // Create bubble
  bubbleActive = true;
  bubbleTimer = 0;
  beep(440, 0.1);
});
restartBtn.addEventListener('click', reset);

// === AUDIO (lazy) =============================================================
function beep(freq, durSec) {
  try {
    if (!audioCtx) audioCtx = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)();
    const osc = audioCtx.createOscillator();
    const gain = audioCtx.createGain();
    osc.type = 'sine';
    osc.frequency.value = freq;
    gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.05, audioCtx.currentTime);
    gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, audioCtx.currentTime + durSec);
    osc.connect(gain);
    gain.connect(audioCtx.destination);
    osc.start();
    osc.stop(audioCtx.currentTime + durSec);
  } catch {}
}

// === UPDATE =============================================================
function update(dt) {
  if (phase !== 'playing') return;
  // Player movement
  const thrust = PLAYER_THRUST;
  if (keys.has('ArrowLeft') || keys.has('a') || keys.has('A')) player.vx -= thrust * dt;
  if (keys.has('ArrowRight') || keys.has('d') || keys.has('D')) player.vx += thrust * dt;
  if (keys.has('ArrowUp') || keys.has('w') || keys.has('W')) player.vy -= thrust * dt;
  if (keys.has('ArrowDown') || keys.has('s') || keys.has('S')) player.vy += thrust * dt;
  // Apply friction
  const friction = Math.pow(PLAYER_FRICTION, dt);
  player.vx *= friction;
  player.vy *= friction;
  // Clamp speed
  const speed = Math.hypot(player.vx, player.vy);
  if (speed > PLAYER_MAX_SPEED) {
    player.vx = (player.vx / speed) * PLAYER_MAX_SPEED;
    player.vy = (player.vy / speed) * PLAYER_MAX_SPEED;
  }
  // Update position
  player.x = Math.min(Math.max(player.x + player.vx * dt, 0), W);
  player.y = Math.min(Math.max(player.y + player.vy * dt, 0), H);

  // Bubble timer
  if (bubbleActive) {
    bubbleTimer += dt;
    if (bubbleTimer >= BUBBLE_DURATION) {
      bubbleActive = false;
      bubbleTimer = 0;
    }
  }

  // Hazard spawning
  hazardSpawnTimer -= dt;
  if (hazardSpawnTimer <= 0) {
    spawnHazard();
    hazardSpawnTimer = 1.0 + Math.random() * 1.0;
  }

  // Update hazards
  for (let i = hazards.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
    const h = hazards[i];
    if (!bubbleActive) {
      h.x += h.vx * dt;
      h.y += h.vy * dt;
    }
    // Remove and score if passed bottom
    if (h.y - h.r > H) {
      hazards.splice(i, 1);
      score += 1;
      beep(600, 0.05);
      continue;
    }
    // Collision with player
    if (phase === 'playing' && h.x - h.r < player.x + player.radius && h.x + h.r > player.x - player.radius && h.y - h.r < player.y + player.radius && h.y + h.r > player.y - player.radius) {
      const dist = Math.hypot(h.x - player.x, h.y - player.y);
      if (dist < h.r + player.radius) {
        phase = 'gameover';
        beep(150, 0.4);
      }
    }
  }
}

// Helper: spawnHazard
function spawnHazard() {
  const r = HAZARD_RADIUS;
  let x, y;
  // Spawn at random x at top, ensure safe radius from player
  let tries = 0;
  do {
    x = Math.random() * W;
    y = -r;
    tries++;
  } while (Math.hypot(x - player.x, y - player.y) < SAFE_RADIUS_AT_SPAWN && tries < 30);
  const speed = HAZARD_SPEED_MIN + Math.random() * (HAZARD_SPEED_MAX - HAZARD_SPEED_MIN);
  hazards.push({ x, y, vx: 0, vy: speed, r });
}

// === RENDER =============================================================
function render() {
  // Draw hazards
  ctx.fillStyle = '#ffb700';
  for (const h of hazards) {
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(h.x, h.y, h.r, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.fill();
  }
  // Draw player
  if (player.alive) {
    ctx.fillStyle = '#7df9ff';
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(player.x, player.y, player.radius, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.fill();
  }
  // HUD
  ctx.fillStyle = '#e6e6f0';
  ctx.font = '16px ui-sans-serif, system-ui';
  ctx.textAlign = 'left';
  ctx.fillText(\`Score \${score}\`, 12, 22);
  // Game over overlay
  if (phase === 'gameover') {
    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(5,5,12,0.65)';
    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
    ctx.textAlign = 'center';
    ctx.font = '44px ui-sans-serif, system-ui';
    ctx.fillStyle = '#ff52ae';
    ctx.fillText('Game Over', W/2, H/2 - 20);
    ctx.font = '20px ui-sans-serif, system-ui';
    ctx.fillStyle = '#e6e6f0';
    ctx.fillText(\`Final score \${score}\`, W/2, H/2 + 14);
    ctx.font = '16px ui-sans-serif, system-ui';
    ctx.fillStyle = '#a5a5b8';
    ctx.fillText('Press R or click Restart', W/2, H/2 + 42);
  }
}

// === OBSERVABLE CONTRACT ===============================================
function publishState() {
  window.__game = {
    state: phase,
    score,
    player: {
      x: player.x,
      y: player.y,
      visible: player.x >= 0 && player.x <= W && player.y >= 0 && player.y <= H,
    },
    objective: 'Create bubbles to freeze obstacles and slip through tight clusters.'
  };
}

// === BOOT ===============================================================
reset();
```

defines:\n\n```\n// === RESET =============================================================\nfunction reset() {\n  player.x = W/2;\n  player.y = H/2;\n  player.vx = 0;\n  player.vy = 0;\n  player.alive = true;\n  hazards = [];\n  score = 0;\n  phase = \"playing\";\n  keys.clear();\n  bubbleActive = false;\n  bubbleTimer = 0;\n  hazardSpawnTimer = 1.5;\n  lastTime = performance.now();\n  startLoop();\n}\n\n// === INPUT =============================================================\ndocument.addEventListener('keydown', (ev) => {\n  if (ev.key === 'r' || ev.key === 'R') { reset(); return; }\n  keys.add(ev.key);\n  if (ev.key === ' ') ev.preventDefault();\n});\ndocument.addEventListener('keyup', (ev) => { keys.delete(ev.key); });\ncanvas.addEventListener('click', (ev) => {\n  // Create bubble\n  bubbleActive = true;\n  bubbleTimer = 0;\n  beep(440, 0.1);\n});\nrestartBtn.addEventListener('click', reset);\n\n// === AUDIO (lazy) =============================================================\nfunction beep(freq, durSec) {\n  try {\n    if (!audioCtx) audioCtx = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)();\n    const osc = audioCtx.createOscillator();\n    const gain = audioCtx.createGain();\n    osc.type = 'sine';\n    osc.frequency.value = freq;\n    gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.05, audioCtx.currentTime);\n    gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, audioCtx.currentTime + durSec);\n    osc.connect(gain);\n    gain.connect(audioCtx.destination);\n    osc.start();\n    osc.stop(audioCtx.currentTime + durSec);\n  } catch {}\n}\n\n// === UPDATE =============================================================\nfunction update(dt) {\n  if (phase !== 'playing') return;\n  // Player movement\n  const thrust = PLAYER_THRUST;\n  if (keys.has('ArrowLeft') || keys.has('a') || keys.has('A')) player.vx -= thrust * dt;\n  if (keys.has('ArrowRight') || keys.has('d') || keys.has('D')) player.vx += thrust * dt;\n  if (keys.has('ArrowUp') || keys.has('w') || keys.has('W')) player.vy -= thrust * dt;\n  if (keys.has('ArrowDown') || keys.has('s') || keys.has('S')) player.vy += thrust * dt;\n  // Apply friction\n  const friction = Math.pow(PLAYER_FRICTION, dt);\n  player.vx *= friction;\n  player.vy *= friction;\n  // Clamp speed\n  const speed = Math.hypot(player.vx, player.vy);\n  if (speed > PLAYER_MAX_SPEED) {\n    player.vx = (player.vx / speed) * PLAYER_MAX_SPEED;\n    player.vy = (player.vy / speed) * PLAYER_MAX_SPEED;\n  }\n  // Update position\n  player.x = Math.min(Math.max(player.x + player.vx * dt, 0), W);\n  player.y = Math.min(Math.max(player.y + player.vy * dt, 0), H);\n\n  // Bubble timer\n  if (bubbleActive) {\n    bubbleTimer += dt;\n    if (bubbleTimer >= BUBBLE_DURATION) {\n      bubbleActive = false;\n      bubbleTimer = 0;\n    }\n  }\n\n  // Hazard spawning\n  hazardSpawnTimer -= dt;\n  if (hazardSpawnTimer <= 0) {\n    spawnHazard();\n    hazardSpawnTimer = 1.0 + Math.random() * 1.0;\n  }\n\n  // Update hazards\n  for (let i = hazards.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {\n    const h = hazards[i];\n    if (!bubbleActive) {\n      h.x += h.vx * dt;\n      h.y += h.vy * dt;\n    }\n    // Remove and score if passed bottom\n    if (h.y - h.r > H) {\n      hazards.splice(i, 1);\n      score += 1;\n      beep(600, 0.05);\n      continue;\n    }\n    // Collision with player\n    if (phase === 'playing' && h.x - h.r < player.x + player.radius && h.x + h.r > player.x - player.radius && h.y - h.r < player.y + player.radius && h.y + h.r > player.y - player.radius) {\n      const dist = Math.hypot(h.x - player.x, h.y - player.y);\n      if (dist < h.r + player.radius) {\n        phase = 'gameover';\n        beep(150, 0.4);\n      }\n    }\n  }\n}\n\n// Helper: spawnHazard\nfunction spawnHazard() {\n  const r = HAZARD_RADIUS;\n  let x, y;\n  // Spawn at random x at top, ensure safe radius from player\n  let tries = 0;\n  do {\n    x = Math.random() * W;\n    y = -r;\n    tries++;\n  } while (Math.hypot(x - player.x, y - player.y) < SAFE_RADIUS_AT_SPAWN && tries < 30);\n  const speed = HAZARD_SPEED_MIN + Math.random() function spawnHazard() {
    let angle = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
    let speed = HAZARD_SPEED_MIN + Math.random() * (HAZARD_SPEED_MAX - HAZARD_SPEED_MIN);
    let r = HAZARD_RADIUS;
    // spawn near random edge?
    let side = Math.floor(Math.random()*4); // 0=left,1=top,2=right,3=bottom
    let x,y;
    if (side===0) { x = 0; y = Math.random()*H; }
    else if (side===1) { x = Math.random()*W; y = 0; }
    else if (side===2) { x = W; y = Math.random()*H; }
    else { x = Math.random()*W; y = H; }
    // ensure safe radius away from player start; might not matter for first spawn.
    hazards.push({ x, y, vx: Math.cos(angle)*speed, vy: Math.sin(angle)*speed, r });
    score += 1; // increase score
    beep(440,0.05);
}

Now create new block:

`
    // === RESET ==============================================================
    function reset() {
      // Reset game state
      player = { x: W/2, y: H/2, vx: 0, vy: 0, radius: 12, alive: true };
      hazards = [];
      score = 0;
      phase = "playing";
      keys = new Set();
      bubbleTimer = 0;
      bubbleCooldown = 0;
      spawnTimer = 1.0;
      publishState();
      startLoop();
    }
    // === INPUT ==============================================================
    // Key handling
    document.addEventListener('keydown', (ev) => {
      if (ev.key === 'r' || ev.key === 'R') { reset(); return; }
      keys.add(ev.key);
    });
    document.addEventListener('keyup', (ev) => {
      keys.delete(ev.key);
    });
    // Restart button
    restartBtn.addEventListener('click', reset);
    // Bubble activation (tap/click)
    canvas.addEventListener('pointerdown', (ev) => {
      if (bubbleCooldown <= 0 && phase === 'playing') {
        bubbleTimer = 0.5; // bubble lasts 0.5 sec
        bubbleCooldown = 1.0; // 1 sec cooldown after use
        beep(440, 0.1);
      }
    });
    // === UPDATE ==============================================================
    function update(dt) {
      if (phase !== 'playing') return;
      // Player movement (arrow keys or WASD)
      const up = keys.has('ArrowUp') || keys.has('w') || keys.has('W');
      const down = keys.has('ArrowDown') || keys.has('s') || keys.has('S');
      const left = keys.has('ArrowLeft') || keys.has('a') || keys.has('A');
      const right = keys.has('ArrowRight') || keys.has('d') || keys.has('D');
      if (up) {
        player.vy -= PLAYER_THRUST * dt;
      }
      if (down) {
        player.vy += PLAYER_THRUST * dt;
      }
      if (left) {
        player.vx -= PLAYER_THRUST * dt;
      }
      if (right) {
        player.vx += PLAYER_THRUST * dt;
      }
      // Apply friction
      const decay = Math.pow(PLAYER_FRICTION, dt);
      player.vx *= decay;
      player.vy *= decay;
      // Clamp speed
      const speed = Math.hypot(player.vx, player.vy);
      if (speed > PLAYER_MAX_SPEED) {
        const scale = PLAYER_MAX_SPEED / speed;
        player.vx *= scale;
        player.vy *= scale;
      }
      // Update position
      player.x += player.vx * dt;
      player.y += player.vy * dt;
      // Keep player within canvas bounds
      if (player.x < 0) player.x = 0;
      if (player.x > W) player.x = W;
      if (player.y < 0) player.y = 0;
      if (player.y > H) player.y = H;
      // Bubble timer
      if (bubbleTimer > 0) bubbleTimer -= dt;
      if (bubbleCooldown > 0) bubbleCooldown -= dt;
      // Hazard spawn timer
      spawnTimer -= dt;
      if (spawnTimer <= 0) {
        spawnTimer = 1.0; // spawn every second
        spawnHazard();
      }
      // Update hazards
      for (let i = hazards.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
        const h = hazards[i];
        // Move hazard
        h.x += h.vx * dt;
        h.y += h.vy * dt;
        // If bubble active and hazard within radius, freeze
        if (bubbleTimer > 0) {
          const dist = Math.hypot(h.x - player.x, h.y - player.y);
          if (dist < player.radius + HAZARD_RADIUS + 30) {
            // freeze - skip movement (already done)
            continue;
          }
        }
        // Remove hazards that leave canvas
        if (h.x < -HAZARD_RADIUS || h.x > W + HAZARD_RADIUS || h.y < -HAZARD_RADIUS || h.y > H + HAZARD_RADIUS) {
          hazards.splice(i, 1);
          continue;
        }
        // Collision with player
        const dx = h.x - player.x;
        const dy = h.y - player.y;
        const dist = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
        if (dist < h.r + player.radius) {
          // Player hit
          player.alive = false;
          phase = 'gameover';
          publishState();
          beep(110, 0.3);
          break;
        }
      }
      // Scoring: increase score per second while playing
      score += dt * 10; // 10 points per second
    }
    // === RENDER ==============================================================
    function render() {
      // Background already cleared
      // Draw hazards
      ctx.fillStyle = '#ffdd33';
      for (const h of hazards) {
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(h.x, h.y, h.r, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();
      }
      // Draw player
      if (player.alive) {
        ctx.fillStyle = '#7df9ff';
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(player.x, player.y, player.radius, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();
      }
      // Draw HUD
      ctx.fillStyle = '#e6e6f0';
      ctx.font = '16px ui-sans-serif, system-ui';
      ctx.textAlign = 'left';
      ctx.fillText('Score ' + Math.floor(score), 12, 22);
    }
    // === AUDIO (lazy) =======================================================
    let audioCtx = null;
    function beep(freq, durSec) {
      try {
        if (!audioCtx) audioCtx = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)();
        const osc = audioCtx.createOscillator();
        const gain = audioCtx.createGain();
        osc.type = 'triangle';
        osc.frequency.value = freq;
        gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.04, audioCtx.currentTime);
        gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, audioCtx.currentTime + durSec);
        osc.connect(gain);
        gain.connect(audioCtx.destination);
        osc.start();
        osc.stop(audioCtx.currentTime + durSec);
      } catch {}
    }
    // === OBSERVABLE CONTRACT ==============================================
    function publishState() {
      window.__game = {
        state: phase,
        score: Math.floor(score),
        player: {
          x: player.x,
          y: player.y,
          visible: player.x >= 0 && player.x < W && player.y >= 0 && player.y < H,
        },
        objective: 'Avoid hazards and use bubbles to slip through clusters.',
      };
    }
    // === BOOT ==============================================================
    reset();
`

Define spawnHazard:

function spawnHazard() {
  // spawn at random edge, moving inward
  const edge = Math.random();
  let x, y, vx, vy;
  if (edge < 0.5) {
    // left or right
    const fromLeft = Math.random() < 0.5;
    x = fromLeft ? -HAZARD_RADIUS : W + HAZARD_RADIUS;
    y = Math.random() * H;
    const speed = HAZARD_SPEED_MIN + Math.random() * (HAZARD_SPEED_MAX - HAZARD_SPEED_MIN);
    vx = fromLeft ? speed : -speed;
    vy = (Math.random() - 0.5) * speed * 0.5;
  } else {
    // top or bottom
    const fromTop = Math.random() < 0.5;
    x = Math.random() * W;
    y = fromTop ? -HAZARD_RADIUS : H + HAZARD_RADIUS;
    const speed = HAZARD_SPEED_MIN + Math.random() * (HAZARD_SPEED_MAX - HAZARD_SPEED_MIN);
    vy = fromTop ? speed : -speed;
    vx = (Math.random() - 0.5) * speed * 0.5;
  }
  const r = HAZARD_RADIUS;
  // Ensure safe radius from player at spawn
  const dx = x - player.x;
  const dy = y - player.y;
  const dist = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
  if (dist < SAFE_RADIUS_AT_SPAWN) {
    // don't spawn this hazard now; just skip
    return;
  }
  hazards.push({ x, y, vx, vy, r });
}

      player.vy += PLAYER_THRUST * dt;\n      }\n      if (left) {\n        player.vx -= PLAYER_THRUST * dt;\n      }\n      if (right) {\n        player.vx += PLAYER_THRUST * dt;\n      }\n      // Apply friction\n      const decay = Math.pow(PLAYER_FRICTION, dt);\n      player.vx *= decay;\n      player.vy *= decay;\n      // Clamp speed\n      const speed = Math.hypot(player.vx, player.vy);\n      if (speed > PLAYER_MAX_SPEED) {\n        const scale = PLAYER_MAX_SPEED / speed;\n        player.vx *= scale;\n        player.vy *= scale;\n      }\n      // Update position\n      player.x += player.vx * dt;\n      player.y += player.vy * dt;\n      // Keep player within canvas bounds\n      if (player.x < 0) player.x = 0;\n      if (player.x > W) player.x = W;\n      if (player.y < 0) player.y = 0;\n      if (player.y > H) player.y = H;\n      // Bubble timer\n      if (bubbleTimer > 0) bubbleTimer -= dt;\n      if (bubbleCooldown > 0) bubbleCooldown -= dt;\n      // Hazard spawn timer\n      spawnTimer -= dt;\n      if (spawnTimer <= 0) {\n        spawnTimer = 1.0; // spawn every second\n        spawnHazard();\n      }\n      // Update hazards\n      for (let i = hazards.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {\n        const h = hazards[i];\n        // Move hazard\n        h.x += h.vx * dt;\n        h.y += h.vy * dt;\n        // If bubble active and hazard within radius, freeze\n        if (bubbleTimer > 0) {\n          const dist = Math.hypot(h.x - player.x, h.y - player.y);\n          if (dist < player.radius + HAZARD_RADIUS + 30) {\n            // freeze - skip movement (already done)\n            continue;\n          }\n        }\n        // Remove hazards that leave canvas\n        if (h.x < -HAZARD_RADIUS || h.x > W + HAZARD_RADIUS || h.y < -HAZARD_RADIUS || h.y > H + HAZARD_RADIUS) {\n          hazards.splice(i, 1);\n          continue;\n        }\n        // Collision with player\n        const dx = h.x - player.x;\n        const dy = h.y - player.y;\n        const dist = Math.hypot(dx, dy);\n        if (dist < h.r + player.radius) {\n          // Player hit\n          player.alive = false;\n          phase = 'gameover';\n          publishState();\n          beep(110, 0.3);\n          break;\n        }\n      }\n      // Scoring: increase score per second while playing\n      score += dt * 10; // 10 points per second\n    }\n    // === RENDER ==============================================================\n    function render() {\n      // Background already cleared\n      // Draw hazards\n      ctx.fillStyle = '#ffdd33';\n      for (const h of hazards) {\n        ctx.beginPath();\n        ctx.arc(h.x, h.y, h.r, 0, Math.PI * 2);\n        ctx.fill();\n      }\n      // Draw player\n      if (player.alive) {\n        ctx.fillStyle = '#7df9ff';\n        ctx.beginPath();\n        ctx.arc(player.x, player.y, player.radius, 0, Math.PI * 2);\n        ctx.fill();\n      }\n      // Draw HUD\n      ctx.fillStyle = '#e6e6f0';\n      ctx.font = '16px ui-sans-serif, system-ui';\n      ctx.textAlign = 'left';\n      ctx.fillText('Score ' + Math.floor(score), 12, 22);\n    }\n    // === AUDIO (lazy) =======================================================\n    let audioCtx = null;\n    function beep(freq, durSec) {\n      try {\n        if (!audioCtx) audioCtx = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)();\n        const osc = audioCtx.createOscillator();\n        const gain = audioCtx.createGain();\n        osc.type = 'triangle';\n        osc.frequency.value = freq;\n        gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.04, audioCtx.currentTime);\n        gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, audioCtx.currentTime + durSec);\n        osc.connect(gain);\n        gain.connect(audioCtx.destination);\n        osc.start();\n        osc.stop(audioCtx.currentTime + durSec);\n      } catch {}\n    }\n    // === OBSERVABLE CONTRACT ==============================================\n    function publishState() {\n      window.__game = {\n        state: phase,\n        score: Math.floor(score),\n        player: {\n          x: player.x,\n          y: player.y,\n          visible: player.x >= 0 && player.x < W && player.y >= 0 && player.y < H,\n        },\n        objective: 'Avoid hazards and use bubbles to slip through clusters.',\n      };\n    }\n    // === BOOT ==============================================================\n    reset();\n`\n\nThat's a lot of code. Define function spawnHazard: pick random angle and speed; ensure not within safe radius.

We'll add:

function spawnHazard() {
   let x, y;
   // spawn at random edge: we can choose random side edges.
   // To keep simple, just spawn anywhere but safe radius.
   let tries = 0;
   do {
     x = Math.random() * W;
     y = Math.random() * H;
     tries++;
   } while (Math.hypot(x - player.x, y - player.y) < SAFE_RADIUS_AT_SPAWN && tries < 30);
   const angle = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
   const speed = HAZARD_SPEED_MIN + Math.random() * (HAZARD_SPEED_MAX - HAZARD_SPEED_MIN);
   hazards.push({ x, y, vx: Math.cos(angle) * speed, vy: Math.sin(angle) * speed, radius: HAZARD_RADIUS });
}

function spawnWave(n) { for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) spawnHazard(); }

Add these in Update or maybe in a separate // === HELPERS? but we can include in any section after loop or before reset. In reference, helper wrap was at bottom after Observable contract. Common interpretations:

- "controls list mismatch" → the `<ul class="game-controls">` in `index.html` and the `controls` array in `meta.json` disagree. Pick one source of truth, edit the other to match exactly.

prompt (15,997 chars)
## Reference example: "asteroids"

The following is a complete, validator-passing game. **Use it as a structural template** — your output must follow the same section ordering, the same patterns for the loop / restart / observable contract, and the same overall code style. Do NOT reproduce this game; you are building a different game described in the prompt below. The example exists only to anchor structure.

Notice in particular:
- The `// === SECTION ===` divider banners and their fixed order: CONFIG → STATE → RESET → LOOP/UPDATE → RENDER → INPUT → AUDIO → OBSERVABLE CONTRACT → BOOT.
- Plain object literals for game state (no `class`).
- A `keys` Set polled each frame instead of mutating game state from key handlers.
- `if (loopId !== null) cancelAnimationFrame(loopId)` at the top of `startLoop()`.
- The animation loop body wrapped in `try/catch`.
- `publishState()` called once per frame at the end of the tick.
- `window.__game` populated honestly (player.visible reflects actual viewport position).
- AudioContext initialized lazily inside `beep()`, wrapped in try/catch.

### Example `meta.json`
```json
{
  "title": "Neon Asteroids",
  "description": "Rotate, thrust, and shoot. Big rocks split into small ones. Get hit and you're gone.",
  "controls": [
    "Left/Right (or A/D) to rotate",
    "Up (or W) to thrust",
    "Space to fire",
    "Press R or click Restart"
  ],
  "tags": ["arcade", "shooter", "physics"]
}
```

### Example `index.html`
```html
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
  <meta charset="utf-8">
  <title>Neon Asteroids</title>
  <style>
    body { margin: 0; background: #0a0a0f; color: #e6e6f0; font-family: ui-sans-serif, system-ui, sans-serif; }
    .game-header { padding: 12px 16px; border-bottom: 1px solid #1d1d2a; }
    .game-header h1 { margin: 0; font-size: 18px; color: #7df9ff; }
    .game-header .game-description { margin: 4px 0; color: #a5a5b8; }
    .game-header .game-controls { margin: 4px 0; padding-left: 18px; color: #c8c8d8; }
    main { display: flex; flex-direction: column; align-items: center; padding: 16px; }
    canvas { background: #05050c; border: 1px solid #2a2f44; }
    .game-restart { margin-top: 10px; padding: 8px 14px; background: #1c2030; color: #7df9ff; border: 1px solid #2a2f44; cursor: pointer; }
    .game-restart:hover { background: #232842; }
  </style>
</head>
<body>
  <header class="game-header">
    <h1>Neon Asteroids</h1>
    <p class="game-description">Rotate, thrust, and shoot. Big rocks split into small ones. Get hit and you're gone.</p>
    <ul class="game-controls">
      <li>Left/Right (or A/D) to rotate</li>
      <li>Up (or W) to thrust</li>
      <li>Space to fire</li>
      <li>Press R or click Restart</li>
    </ul>
  </header>
  <main>
    <canvas id="game" width="800" height="600"></canvas>
    <button id="restart" class="game-restart" type="button">Restart</button>
  </main>
  <script>
  // === CONFIG =============================================================
  const W = 800, H = 600;
  const SHIP_RADIUS = 12;
  const SHIP_THRUST = 280;       // px/sec^2
  const SHIP_FRICTION = 0.6;     // velocity decay per second (1 = none)
  const SHIP_TURN = Math.PI * 1.8; // rad/sec
  const BULLET_SPEED = 520;      // px/sec
  const BULLET_LIFE = 1.0;       // sec
  const FIRE_COOLDOWN = 0.20;    // sec between shots
  const ASTEROID_BIG_R = 38;
  const ASTEROID_MID_R = 22;
  const ASTEROID_LIL_R = 12;
  const ASTEROID_SPEED_RANGE = [40, 110];
  const SAFE_RADIUS_AT_SPAWN = 160; // no asteroid spawns inside this disk

  const SCORE_BIG = 20, SCORE_MID = 50, SCORE_LIL = 100;

  // === STATE ==============================================================
  const canvas = document.getElementById("game");
  const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");
  const restartBtn = document.getElementById("restart");

  let ship;        // { x, y, vx, vy, angle, alive }
  let bullets;     // [{ x, y, vx, vy, life }]
  let asteroids;   // [{ x, y, vx, vy, r, shape: number[] }]
  let score;
  let highScore = 0;
  let phase;       // "playing" | "gameover"
  let fireCooldown;
  let keys;
  let lastTime;
  let loopId = null;
  let audioCtx = null;

  function reset() {
    ship = { x: W / 2, y: H / 2, vx: 0, vy: 0, angle: -Math.PI / 2, alive: true };
    bullets = [];
    asteroids = [];
    spawnWave(5);
    score = 0;
    phase = "playing";
    fireCooldown = 0;
    keys = new Set();
    lastTime = performance.now();
    publishState();
    startLoop();
  }

  function startLoop() {
    if (loopId !== null) cancelAnimationFrame(loopId);
    function tick(now) {
      try {
        const dt = Math.min((now - lastTime) / 1000, 0.1);
        lastTime = now;
        update(dt);
        render();
        publishState();
      } catch (err) {
        console.error("loop error", err);
      }
      loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
    }
    loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
  }

  function spawnWave(n) {
    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) spawnAsteroid(ASTEROID_BIG_R);
  }

  function spawnAsteroid(r) {
    let x, y, tries = 0;
    do {
      x = Math.random() * W;
      y = Math.random() * H;
      tries++;
    } while (Math.hypot(x - ship.x, y - ship.y) < SAFE_RADIUS_AT_SPAWN && tries < 30);
    const angle = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
    const speed = ASTEROID_SPEED_RANGE[0] + Math.random() * (ASTEROID_SPEED_RANGE[1] - ASTEROID_SPEED_RANGE[0]);
    asteroids.push({
      x, y,
      vx: Math.cos(angle) * speed,
      vy: Math.sin(angle) * speed,
      r,
      shape: makeAsteroidShape(),
    });
  }

  function makeAsteroidShape() {
    // Per-vertex radius multipliers, 8-12 vertices for a knobbly silhouette.
    const n = 8 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 5);
    const out = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) out.push(0.75 + Math.random() * 0.45);
    return out;
  }

  // === UPDATE =============================================================
  function update(dt) {
    if (phase !== "playing") return;

    // Ship.
    if (ship.alive) {
      const turnIn = (keys.has("ArrowRight") || keys.has("d") || keys.has("D") ? 1 : 0)
                   - (keys.has("ArrowLeft")  || keys.has("a") || keys.has("A") ? 1 : 0);
      ship.angle += turnIn * SHIP_TURN * dt;

      const thrusting = keys.has("ArrowUp") || keys.has("w") || keys.has("W");
      if (thrusting) {
        ship.vx += Math.cos(ship.angle) * SHIP_THRUST * dt;
        ship.vy += Math.sin(ship.angle) * SHIP_THRUST * dt;
      }
      // Mild friction so the ship doesn't drift forever.
      const decay = Math.pow(SHIP_FRICTION, dt);
      ship.vx *= decay;
      ship.vy *= decay;
      ship.x = wrap(ship.x + ship.vx * dt, W);
      ship.y = wrap(ship.y + ship.vy * dt, H);

      fireCooldown -= dt;
      if ((keys.has(" ") || keys.has("Space")) && fireCooldown <= 0) {
        fire();
        fireCooldown = FIRE_COOLDOWN;
      }
    }

    // Bullets.
    for (const b of bullets) {
      b.life -= dt;
      b.x = wrap(b.x + b.vx * dt, W);
      b.y = wrap(b.y + b.vy * dt, H);
    }
    bullets = bullets.filter((b) => b.life > 0);

    // Asteroids.
    for (const a of asteroids) {
      a.x = wrap(a.x + a.vx * dt, W);
      a.y = wrap(a.y + a.vy * dt, H);
    }

    // Bullet-asteroid collisions.
    for (let i = asteroids.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
      const a = asteroids[i];
      let hit = false;
      for (let j = bullets.length - 1; j >= 0; j--) {
        const b = bullets[j];
        if (Math.hypot(a.x - b.x, a.y - b.y) < a.r) {
          bullets.splice(j, 1);
          hit = true;
          break;
        }
      }
      if (hit) splitAsteroid(i);
    }

    // Ship-asteroid collisions.
    if (ship.alive) {
      for (const a of asteroids) {
        if (Math.hypot(a.x - ship.x, a.y - ship.y) < a.r + SHIP_RADIUS * 0.6) {
          ship.alive = false;
          phase = "gameover";
          if (score > highScore) highScore = score;
          beep(110, 0.3);
          break;
        }
      }
    }

    // Wave clear -> spawn next.
    if (asteroids.length === 0 && phase === "playing") {
      spawnWave(5);
    }
  }

  function fire() {
    bullets.push({
      x: ship.x + Math.cos(ship.angle) * SHIP_RADIUS,
      y: ship.y + Math.sin(ship.angle) * SHIP_RADIUS,
      vx: ship.vx + Math.cos(ship.angle) * BULLET_SPEED,
      vy: ship.vy + Math.sin(ship.angle) * BULLET_SPEED,
      life: BULLET_LIFE,
    });
    beep(820, 0.04);
  }

  function splitAsteroid(idx) {
    const a = asteroids[idx];
    asteroids.splice(idx, 1);
    if (a.r === ASTEROID_BIG_R) {
      score += SCORE_BIG;
      for (let i = 0; i < 2; i++) addChild(a, ASTEROID_MID_R);
      beep(440, 0.06);
    } else if (a.r === ASTEROID_MID_R) {
      score += SCORE_MID;
      for (let i = 0; i < 2; i++) addChild(a, ASTEROID_LIL_R);
      beep(560, 0.05);
    } else {
      score += SCORE_LIL;
      beep(700, 0.05);
    }
  }

  function addChild(parent, r) {
    const angle = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
    const speed = ASTEROID_SPEED_RANGE[0] + Math.random() * (ASTEROID_SPEED_RANGE[1] - ASTEROID_SPEED_RANGE[0]);
    asteroids.push({
      x: parent.x, y: parent.y,
      vx: Math.cos(angle) * speed,
      vy: Math.sin(angle) * speed,
      r,
      shape: makeAsteroidShape(),
    });
  }

  // === RENDER =============================================================
  function render() {
    ctx.fillStyle = "#05050c";
    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);

    // Asteroids.
    ctx.strokeStyle = "#a5a5b8";
    ctx.lineWidth = 1.5;
    for (const a of asteroids) drawAsteroid(a);

    // Bullets.
    ctx.fillStyle = "#ffd23f";
    for (const b of bullets) {
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(b.x, b.y, 2.4, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.fill();
    }

    // Ship.
    if (ship.alive) drawShip();

    // HUD.
    ctx.fillStyle = "#e6e6f0";
    ctx.font = "16px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
    ctx.textAlign = "left";
    ctx.fillText(`Score ${score}`, 12, 22);
    ctx.textAlign = "right";
    ctx.fillText(`Best ${highScore}`, W - 12, 22);

    if (phase === "gameover") {
      ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(5,5,12,0.65)";
      ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
      ctx.textAlign = "center";
      ctx.font = "44px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
      ctx.fillStyle = "#ff52ae";
      ctx.fillText("Game Over", W / 2, H / 2 - 20);
      ctx.fillStyle = "#e6e6f0";
      ctx.font = "20px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
      ctx.fillText(`Final score ${score}`, W / 2, H / 2 + 14);
      ctx.fillStyle = "#a5a5b8";
      ctx.font = "16px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
      ctx.fillText("Press R or click Restart", W / 2, H / 2 + 42);
    }
  }

  function drawShip() {
    ctx.save();
    ctx.translate(ship.x, ship.y);
    ctx.rotate(ship.angle);
    ctx.strokeStyle = "#7df9ff";
    ctx.lineWidth = 2;
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(SHIP_RADIUS, 0);
    ctx.lineTo(-SHIP_RADIUS * 0.9, SHIP_RADIUS * 0.7);
    ctx.lineTo(-SHIP_RADIUS * 0.5, 0);
    ctx.lineTo(-SHIP_RADIUS * 0.9, -SHIP_RADIUS * 0.7);
    ctx.closePath();
    ctx.stroke();
    if (keys && (keys.has("ArrowUp") || keys.has("w") || keys.has("W"))) {
      ctx.strokeStyle = "#ffd23f";
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.moveTo(-SHIP_RADIUS * 0.5, 0);
      ctx.lineTo(-SHIP_RADIUS - 6 - Math.random() * 4, 0);
      ctx.stroke();
    }
    ctx.restore();
  }

  function drawAsteroid(a) {
    ctx.beginPath();
    for (let i = 0; i < a.shape.length; i++) {
      const t = (i / a.shape.length) * Math.PI * 2;
      const r = a.r * a.shape[i];
      const x = a.x + Math.cos(t) * r;
      const y = a.y + Math.sin(t) * r;
      if (i === 0) ctx.moveTo(x, y); else ctx.lineTo(x, y);
    }
    ctx.closePath();
    ctx.stroke();
  }

  // === INPUT ==============================================================
  document.addEventListener("keydown", (ev) => {
    if (ev.key === "r" || ev.key === "R") { reset(); return; }
    keys.add(ev.key);
    if (ev.key === " ") ev.preventDefault();
  });
  document.addEventListener("keyup", (ev) => { keys.delete(ev.key); });
  restartBtn.addEventListener("click", reset);

  // === AUDIO (lazy) =======================================================
  function beep(freq, durSec) {
    try {
      if (!audioCtx) audioCtx = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)();
      const osc = audioCtx.createOscillator();
      const gain = audioCtx.createGain();
      osc.type = "triangle";
      osc.frequency.value = freq;
      gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.04, audioCtx.currentTime);
      gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, audioCtx.currentTime + durSec);
      osc.connect(gain); gain.connect(audioCtx.destination);
      osc.start(); osc.stop(audioCtx.currentTime + durSec);
    } catch {}
  }

  // === OBSERVABLE CONTRACT ===============================================
  function publishState() {
    window.__game = {
      state: phase,                                  // "playing" | "gameover"
      score,
      player: {
        x: ship.x,
        y: ship.y,
        // wrap() keeps coords in [0, W) and [0, H), so visible is always true
        // — but assert it explicitly so the contract is honest.
        visible: ship.x >= 0 && ship.x < W && ship.y >= 0 && ship.y < H,
      },
      objective: "Shoot the asteroids before they hit your ship. Big rocks split into smaller ones.",
    };
  }

  // === HELPERS ============================================================
  function wrap(v, max) {
    if (v < 0) return v + max;
    if (v >= max) return v - max;
    return v;
  }

  // === BOOT ===============================================================
  reset();
  </script>
</body>
</html>
```

let canvas = document.getElementById('game');
let ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
let lastTime = performance.now();
let loopId = null;

function startLoop() {
  if (loopId !== null) cancelAnimationFrame(loopId);
  function tick(now) {
    try {
      const dt = Math.min((now - lastTime) / 1000, 0.1);
      lastTime = now;
      // clear
      ctx.clearRect(0, 0, W, H);
      // update & render could be called later (maybe stub)
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('loop error', err);
    }
    loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
  }
  loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
}

prompt (5,156 chars)
Read CONVENTIONS.md and DESIGN.md in your working directory before doing anything else.

Your job in this stage is to break the implementation into a numbered list of small, ordered tasks. The next stage will work through this list one task at a time, in a single LLM session, reloading the page after each task and capturing console errors for you to fix.

Title: Temporal Bubble
Concept: Tap creates a short‑lived bubble around the dot that freezes all obstacles inside it for half a second, letting you slip through tight clusters; the bubble has a cooldown to prevent spamming.

**You must produce one output file: `TASKS.md` in the working directory.** Use your write/edit tool to create this file on disk. After your turn ends, the validator runs `readFile("TASKS.md")` — if the file does not exist on disk, validation fails and your work is wasted. **Do NOT reply with the task list as inline chat text.** The list must be written to the file.

**Tool calls MUST be emitted as actual function/tool invocations — NOT described or simulated inside your reasoning/analysis text.** Writing tool-shaped JSON like `{"filePath":"TASKS.md","content":"..."}` *inside your chain-of-thought* does NOT invoke any tool. It is just text in the analysis channel that nothing reads or executes. The same goes for `{"pattern":"**/*"}` etc. — those only do something when emitted as a real `tool_use` event from the action channel, never when written into your thinking. Several prior runs have failed by hallucinating tool calls inside reasoning; do not repeat that pattern. If at any point you "describe" a tool call in your thinking instead of invoking it, you must immediately invoke the real tool afterward. The format MUST be a numbered list, one task per line, each line starting with `<number>.`:

```
1. <Task title> — <one-sentence description of what to do>
2. <Task title> — <one-sentence description>
...
```

The next stage parses tasks by matching `^\d+\.` at the start of each line. Don't use bullet sub-lists, don't number sub-tasks (1.1, 1.2). One number, one task, one line.

### What makes a good task

- **Small enough** to complete in a single coding turn (~50–150 lines of edits).
- **Testable**: after this task, what does the page do that it didn't before?
- **Leaves the file runnable**: at the end of the task, no syntax errors, no uncaught exceptions on load. Even if the game isn't playable yet, it must load.
- **Specific**: don't say "implement player". Say "implement player movement: arrow keys set keys.left/right; in update(), apply acceleration in the pressed direction with cap at MAX_SPEED, friction otherwise; clamp x to canvas bounds."
- **Concrete numbers**: this is where pixel sizes, ms intervals, and px/sec speeds live. Pick them. The implement stage will use them verbatim.

### Required coverage (in roughly this order)

Cover at minimum, ordered as below. Add tasks if the design demands more (powerups, level progression, screen wrap, etc):

1. **HTML scaffold + meta.json**: header (h1, description, controls list), canvas, restart button per CONVENTIONS.md. Stub `<script>` block (empty). meta.json matching header text.
2. **Game state objects**: player, hazards, score, phase using plain object literals at the top of the script. Set initial values.
3. **requestAnimationFrame loop**: startLoop() with `cancelAnimationFrame(loopId)` discipline at the top. Body wrapped in try/catch. Calls update(dt) and render(). Don't draw anything yet besides clearing the canvas.
4. **Input**: a `keys` Set populated by document keydown/keyup. R-key restart hook. Restart button click hook. Both call the same reset() function.
5. **Player movement**: dt-based physics tied to the key inputs from task 4. Verify each control listed in meta.json actually changes a value.
6. **Hazards**: data structure, spawn rules with a SAFE_RADIUS so nothing spawns on top of the player at t=0, motion (dt-scaled).
7. **Collision detection**: player vs hazards. On hit, transition phase to "gameover" and stop the active gameplay.
8. **Score**: discrete events that increase score, drawn on canvas at top-left.
9. **Render**: clear canvas, draw background, hazards, player, HUD. Player must be the highest-contrast object on screen.
10. **Game-over overlay**: dim background, "Game Over", final score, "Press R or click Restart".
11. **Observable contract**: populate `window.__game = { state, score, player:{x,y,visible}, objective }` every frame. `state` is "playing" | "gameover". `objective` is a 10–200 char single-sentence description.
12. **Audio**: lazy-init AudioContext inside a beep() helper, wrapped in try/catch. Hook into hit / score / death events.

You may merge two trivial tasks if they truly belong together (e.g. tasks 1+2 if state is just three lines). You may split a complex task in two. Aim for 10–15 tasks total.

**Final check before you stop**: confirm you actually called your write/edit tool with `TASKS.md` as the path. If you only described the task list in chat without invoking the tool, the file will not exist on disk and validation will fail. After the file is written, stop. Do not begin implementation.

3. Implement the main loop — set up startLoop function, cancelAnimationFrame handling, requestAnimationFrame loop with try/catch, clear canvas.
4. Set up input handling — create keys Set, keydown/keyup listeners, restart button click and R key to call reset().
5. Implement player movement — dt‑based physics using arrow keys, velocity caps, friction, keep player within canvas bounds.
6. Create hazard system — data structure, spawn logic respecting SAFE_RADIUS, dt‑scaled motion.
7. Add collision detection — detect player vs hazards, transition to "gameover" phase and stop gameplay.
8. Implement scoring — increase score on hazard passes or events, render score on canvas.
9. Render game elements — draw background, hazards, player (high‑contrast), HUD each frame.
10. Add game‑over overlay — dim background, display "Game Over", final score, and restart instructions.
11. Expose observable contract — populate window.__game each frame with state, score, player position/visibility, objective text.
12. Add audio hooks — lazy‑init AudioContext, beep() helper, play sounds on hit, score, death events.
